+apiVersion: argoproj.io/v1alpha1
+kind: ApplicationSet
+metadata:
+  name: goldpinger
+  namespace: goldpinger
+  annotations:
+    argocd.argoproj.io/sync-wave: "7"
+spec:
+  goTemplate: true
+  goTemplateOptions: ["missingkey=error"]
+  generators:
+  - clusters:
+      selector:
+        matchLabels:
+          environment: "cce-prod-tb01"
+          argocd_shard: "0"
+      values:
+        base_domain: '{{index .metadata.annotations "base_domain"}}'
+        env: '{{index .metadata.labels "environment"}}'
+  template:
+    metadata:
+      name: goldpinger
+    spec:
+      project: infra
+      sources:
+        - chart: goldpinger
+          repoURL: https://bloomberg.github.io/goldpinger
+          targetRevision: 1.2.0
+          helm:
+            releaseName: goldpinger
+            valueFiles:
+              - $helm/{{.values.env}}/goldpinger/goldpinger-values.yaml
+            valuesObject:
+              httpRoute:
+                hostnames:
+                  - "goldpinger-tb01.{{.values.base_domain}}"
+        - repoURL: https://github.com/nixys/nxs-universal-chart.git
+          targetRevision: v3.0.18
+          path: .
+          helm:
+            releaseName: goldpinger-nxs
+            valueFiles:
+              - $helm/{{.values.env}}/goldpinger/goldpinger-nxs-values.yaml
+            valuesObject:
+              goldpinger_hostname: "goldpinger-tb01.{{.values.base_domain}}"
+              sso_hostname: "sso-tb01.{{.values.base_domain}}"
+        - repoURL: https://git.sberdevices.ru/gigabb/infra/cowork/helm_values_infra.git
+          targetRevision: master
+          ref: helm
+      destination:
+        server: https://kubernetes.default.svc
+        namespace: goldpinger
+      syncPolicy:
+        syncOptions:
+        - RespectIgnoreDifferences=true
+        - PruneLast=true
+        - ServerSideApply=true
+      revisionHistoryLimit: 3
